- **reskin-style**: Apply a screenshot's or style's whole look to your app, without moving anything. ([RuBNouS/reskin-skill](https://github.com/RuBNouS/reskin-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/reskin-style))
- **reskin-to-template-converter**: Rebuilds an off-brand deck on the approved template, recolored and retyped, preserving the content while changing the skin. ([andreworia/claude-pptx-skills](https://github.com/andreworia/claude-pptx-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reskin-to-template-converter) · ★4)
- **reslicing**: Changing a backlog that already exists: what triggers a re-slice, what may be touched at each ticket status and what may never be, finding affected tickets when a changed ruling leaves no citation to follow, the four outcomes an affected ticket can have, when merging is and is not allowed, and sweeping tickets a ruling has invalidated. Use when: a spec changed, an architectural or convention ruling changed, a review produced findings, a cap moved, or a slice proved wrong mid-flight — and existing tickets have to change. ([odenizturker/app-dev-agents](https://github.com/odenizturker/app-dev-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/reslicing))
- **reslop**: Rewrite docstrings and comments by reading the actual implementation, call sites, and tests — produce grounded, factual prose instead of deleting slop. Sibling of /techne:deslop. Use when the user wants to replace overhyped or hallucinated documentation with accurate one- or two-line descriptions derived from what the code actually does. ([ajbarea/techne](https://github.com/ajbarea/techne/tree/HEAD/plugins/techne/skills/reslop) · ★1)
- **resmi-belge-ve-sicile-guven-tmk-7**: Tapu, nüfus, ticaret sicili gibi resmî sicillere veya resmî senetlere dayanılan ya da bunların içeriğine itiraz edilen durumlarda; resmî belgenin ispat gücünü ve aksinin nasıl ispatlanacağını belirlemek için kullanılır. ([aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude](https://github.com/aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude/tree/HEAD/medeni-hukuk-baslangic/skills/resmi-belge-ve-sicile-guven-tmk-7) · ★25)
- **resmi-belge-ve-sicile-guven-tmk-7**: Tapu, nüfus, ticaret sicili gibi resmî sicillere veya resmî senetlere dayanılan ya da bunların içeriğine itiraz edilen durumlarda; resmî belgenin ispat gücünü ve aksinin nasıl ispatlanacağını belirlem ([mesutcandemir39/turkiye-legal](https://github.com/mesutcandemir39/turkiye-legal/tree/HEAD/medeni-hukuk-baslangic/skills/resmi-belge-ve-sicile-guven-tmk-7))
- **resmi-eposta**: Türkçe kurumsal ve resmi e-posta yazar ya da mevcut taslağı düzeltir. Kullanıcı "e-posta yaz", "mail taslağı", "resmi mail", "müşteriye/müdüre/hocama mail", "toplantı talebi maili" gibi bir istekte bulunduğunda kullan. ([maderinmad/turkce-claude-skills](https://github.com/maderinmad/turkce-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/resmi-eposta) · ★1)
- **resmi-yazi**: Türkçe resmi yazı, dilekçe, itiraz mektubu ve kurumsal yazışma üretimi. Devlet kurumlarına dilekçe, SGK/vergi idaresi yazışması, Noter belgesi, şikâyet mektubu, iş başvurusu için kullan. ([aiskillstore/marketplace](https://github.com/aiskillstore/marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/cesareth/resmi-yazi) · ★487 · 4 copies)
- **resmi-yazi**: Türkçe resmi yazı, dilekçe ve üst yazı hazırlama. Doğru hitap, başlık, format ve kapanış ile kurumsal/resmi yazışmalar üretir. Use when the user needs a formal Turkish letter, petition (dilekçe), official cover letter (üst yazı), or correspondence to a public institution or company. ([eruo005-dev/turkce-claude-skills](https://github.com/eruo005-dev/turkce-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/resmi-yazi))
- **resmi-yazisma**: Resmi ve kurumsal Türkçe yazışma üretir. Dilekçe, resmi e-posta, kurum içi yazı, başvuru, itiraz, bilgi talebi. Net, nesnel ve saygılı; tumturaklı ve boş dolgudan arınmış. WHEN "resmi yazı yaz", "dilekçe yaz", "resmi e-posta", "kuruma başvuru", "itiraz dilekçesi", "resmi dille", "bilgi talebi", "kurum içi yazışma", "üst yazı". ([kivancakdeniz/turkce-yazim-stilleri](https://github.com/kivancakdeniz/turkce-yazim-stilleri/tree/HEAD/resmi-yazisma))
- **resnest**: Use ResNeSt split-attention CNN models across PyTorch, optional MXNet Gluon, and optional Detectron2 backbones with safe install checks, model routing, and troubleshooting. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/resnest) · ★357 · 2 copies)
- **resnet-architecture-modification-for-dimensionality-control**: Use when your task requires a pretrained convolutional encoder (ResNet18) to produce fixed-size representation vectors of a specific dimensionality (e.g., 512 dimensions) rather than the default output size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/resnet-architecture-modification-for-dimensionality-control))
- **resnet-training**: Guides Tencent ML-Images TensorFlow 1.x ResNet graph, pretraining, finetuning, flags, and training troubleshooting. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/tencent-ml-images/sub-skills/resnet-training) · ★357)
- **resnet-transfer-learning-inference**: Use when ion images from METASPACE-style imaging MS datasets must be classified as off-sample vs on-sample with a pretrained ResNet-50, or when reproducing the paper's off-sample frequency counts over DESI datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/resnet-transfer-learning-inference))
- **resnet-transfer-training**: Use when quality-controlled SAM/BAM alignments have already been encoded into 100x100-pixel deletion-region images and zero-padded base sequences (length 100), and a fused deep model must be trained to call deletion variants. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/resnet-transfer-training))
- **resnik-semantic-similarity-of-go-terms**: Use when chromosome clusters (e.g. BHi-Cect bottom enclaves from IMR90 Hi-C data) need an ontology-based functional similarity score to correlate against BPT distance or genomic coordinate distance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/resnik-semantic-similarity-of-go-terms))
- **resolucao-rescisao-contratual**: Redige a acao para desfazer contrato civil — distingue resolucao por inadimplemento (CC 474-475 — clausula resolutiva expressa opera de pleno direito, tacita exige interpelacao judicial; parte lesada pede resolucao + perdas e danos), resilicao por vontade (distrato 472, denuncia unilateral 473) e resolucao por onerosidade excessiva (CC 478-480). Inclui excecao de contrato nao cumprido (476-477). Use quando o operador disser rescindir contrato, resolver contrato, distrato, inadimplemento contratual, quebra de contrato, ou desfazer um contrato civil. ([sbroggioadv/civel-adv-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/civel-adv-os-marketplace/tree/HEAD/civel-adv-os/skills/resolucao-rescisao-contratual) · ★7)

- **resolucion-por-incumplimiento**: Prepara en Word la comunicación que da por resuelto un contrato por incumplimiento (art. 1124 CC o cláusula resolutoria expresa) y un dictamen breve con jurisprudencia de la Sala Primera, para quien resuelve o para quien recibe la resolución.
